M^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha,\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1}}
entering into the canonical transformation of color charges
Q^{\hskip 0.85355pta}_{\alpha}
, Eq. (
3.7
):

M^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1}}=i\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha_{2}}\,\frac{{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}}{\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\phantom{l}}_{\alpha_{1}\!}\cdot{\mathbf{k}}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}}\,.

(4.3)

Furthermore, the requirement to exclude third-order terms in the Hamiltonian
H^{(3)}
, containing the vertex functions
{\mathcal{V}}^{\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
and
{\mathcal{U}}^{\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
leads to the following expressions for the coefficient functions
V^{\hskip 0.56917pt(1,3\hskip 0.56917pt|\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha,\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
in the canonical transformation (
3.6
):

\begin{split}&V^{\hskip 0.56917pt(1\hskip 0.56917pt|\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha,\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=-\hskip 0.56917pt\displaystyle\frac{{\mathcal{V}}^{\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}}{\bigl(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\phantom{l}}_{\alpha\!}\cdot{\mathbf{k}}\bigr)-\bigl(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\phantom{l}}_{\alpha_{1}\!}\cdot{\mathbf{k}}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\bigr)-\bigl(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\phantom{l}}_{\alpha_{2}\!}\cdot{\mathbf{k}}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\bigr)}\,\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}),\\[17.22217pt]
&V^{\hskip 0.56917pt(3\hskip 0.56917pt|\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha,\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=-\hskip 0.56917pt\displaystyle\frac{{\mathcal{U}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt*\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}}{\bigl(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\phantom{l}}_{\alpha\!}\cdot{\mathbf{k}}\bigr)+\bigl(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\phantom{l}}_{\alpha_{1}\!}\cdot{\mathbf{k}}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\bigr)+\bigl(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\phantom{l}}_{\alpha_{2}\!}\cdot{\mathbf{k}}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\bigr)}\,\delta({\bf k}+{\bf k}_{1}+{\bf k}_{2}).\end{split}

Note that the denominators of these expressions for arbitrary values
\alpha,\,\alpha_{1}
and
\alpha_{2}
explicitly contain wave vectors, unlike, for example, similar expressions in
[
42
,
1
]
.
The coefficient
V^{\hskip 0.56917pt(2\hskip 0.56917pt|\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha,\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
is found from Eq. (
3.8
).

Thus, instead of the sum of the initial Hamiltonians
H^{(0)}+H^{(3)}
, Eq. (
4.1
), we now obtain a new free-field Hamiltonian
{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)}
for noninteracting plasmons in terms of the new normal variables
c^{\ast\ \!\!(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and
c^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!\!(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
:

{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)}=\sum_{\alpha}\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\phantom{l}}_{\alpha\!}\cdot{\mathbf{k}})\ \!c^{\ast\ \!\!(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}.

(4.4)

Hereinafter, the Hamiltonians in the new variables will be designated by the calligraphic letter
{\mathcal{H}}
.

Furthermore, we can move to the construction of an explicit form of effective
fifth-order
Hamiltonian
{\mathcal{H}}^{(5)}
, which describes
plasmon bremsstrahlung radiation produced by the interaction between two hard test color-charged particles
4
4

4

As part of the formal rule of counting degrees of nonlinearity we have adopted, this Hamiltonian has a higher degree of nonlinearity than all the Hamiltonians we have considered so far
[
42
,
43
,
1
]
and describes a qualitatively new physical process.
. For this purpose, we need to collect all contributions proportional to the products
c^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}

and

c^{\ast\ \!\!(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}

from the free-field Hamiltonian
H^{(0)}
, Eq. (
2.9
), and from the interaction Hamiltonian
H^{(3)}
, Eq. (
2.11
), to be generated by the canonical transformations (
3.6
) and (
3.7
). Thereby we obtain the effective fifth-order Hamiltonian describing plasmon bremsstrahlung:

{\mathcal{H}}^{(5)}=\sum_{\rho}\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\,+\,\sum_{\rho}\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,c^{\ast\ \!\!(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}.

(4.5)

Here, the effective amplitude
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)
has the following structure:

{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)=\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t),

(4.6)

where, in turn, we represent the ‘‘density’’ of the amplitude
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
as the sum of two parts that differ in physical content

{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)={T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)+{T}^{\,{\rm II}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t).

(4.7)

The first part, taking into account (
4.2
) and (
4.3
), has the following structure:

{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{\rho}\cdot{\bf k}}\,\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\times\vskip-8.5359pt

(4.8)

\displaystyle\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt

\displaystyle{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}(t)\,+\,{\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}(t)\,-\,

\displaystyle{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}(t)\,-\,{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}(t)\Bigr],

while the second part can be represented as

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt{T}^{\,{\rm II}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)={\mathcal{V}}^{\,\ast\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\times

(4.9)

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\biggr)\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)-{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)\Bigr]\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 150.79968pt2\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{{\bf q},\,{\bf q}-{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\times

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf q}-{\bf k})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf q}-{\bf k})}\biggr)\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\ast\,(2)}_{\,{\bf q}-{\bf k}}(t)-{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\ast\,(1)}_{\,{\bf q}-{\bf k}}(t)\Bigr]\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 159.3356pt{\mathcal{U}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\times

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf k}+{\bf q})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf k}+{\bf q})}\biggr)\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(2)}_{\,-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)-{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(1)}_{\,-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)\Bigr].

We see that only the first part of the total amplitude
{T}^{\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
, namely
{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
, actually depends on the ‘‘index’’
\rho
. The effective amplitude
{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
describes the Compton-like plasmon bremsstrahlung. Figure
2
gives the diagrammatic interpretation of different terms in (
4.8
) (more exactly, for the complex conjugate expression
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm I}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
).

\!\!\!\!\!{\mathbf{+}}

Figure 2:
Diagrammatic representation of the first and last terms in square brackets in the complex conjugate effective amplitude
{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
, Eq. (
4.8
). The blob stands for the HTL-resummation and the double line denotes a hard particle

The plasmon bremsstrahlung here comes from either hard particle 1 or 2. This is reflected in the multiplier with the index
\rho=1,2
in (
4.8
).
The outgoing wave lines in fig.
2
correspond to the components
c^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
of the total normal variable
c^{\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}
, the horizontal double line between two interaction vertices corresponds to the ‘‘propagator’’ of the hard particles

\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}\,-\,{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{\rho}\!\cdot{\bf k}}

(4.10)

and the interaction vertices, defining plasmon bremsstrahlung, correspond to the vertex functions
{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}(t)
or
{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}(t)
. The remaining vertex functions of the form
{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)
and
{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)
are related to the exchange process of an intermediate ‘‘virtual’’ oscillation to which the sum in parentheses in (
4.8
) corresponds.

The effective amplitude
{T}^{\,{\rm II}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
, Eq. (
4.9
), determines the processes, shown in fig.
3
. It is these processes that represent the transition bremsstrahlung, which was discussed in the Introduction.

{}{}{}{}{}\hskip 28.45274pt{\mathbf{+}}

{}{}{}{}{}\hskip 28.45274pt{\mathbf{+}}

Figure 3:
Diagrammatic representation of contributions related to the three-plasmon vertex function
{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
in the complex conjugate expression of the effective amplitude
{T}^{\,{\rm II}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
, Eq. (
4.9
). There are also the contribution connected with another three-plasmon vertex function

{\mathcal{U}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}

The graphs in fig.
3
are connected with the interaction of hard particles with plasmons through the three-plasmon vertex functions
{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
,
{\mathcal{V}}^{\,\ast\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
and
{\mathcal{U}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
with the intermediate ‘‘virtual’’ oscillations to which the factors
in parentheses in (
4.9
) correspond. We have depicted the contributions of the various terms in (
4.9
), with different directions of momenta in the internal lines. Unlike the previous amplitude
{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
here the outgoing wave lines correspond to the full normal variable
c^{\!\!\!\!\!\!\phantom{(1)}a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}=c^{\,(1)\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}+c^{\,(2)\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}
. It is clear why the second effective amplitude
{T}^{\,{\rm II}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
does not depend on the
\rho
index: particles 1 and 2 enter symmetrically into this radiation process. Finally, the interaction vertices
{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)
,
{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)
etc. correspond to interaction of ‘‘virtual’’ plasmons with hard particles.

5
Multiple-time-scale perturbation expansion

Now we turn to the construction of a kinetic equation describing the process of plasmon bremsstrahlung produced by the color charge rotation of hard particles passing through the hot QCD matter. As the interaction Hamiltonian we consider the effective Hamiltonian
{\mathcal{H}}^{(5)}
, Eq. (
4.5
). The equations of motion for the components of the bosonic normal variables
c^{\phantom{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}\!\!a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and
c^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and the color charges
\mathcal{Q}^{\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}
are defined by the corresponding Hamilton equations. For the soft Bose-excitations we find

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.42271pt\Bigl\{c^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{H}}^{(5)}\Bigr\}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2},

(5.1)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\ast\ \!\!(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.42271pt\Bigl\{c^{\ast\ \!\!(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{H}}^{(5)}\Bigr\}=i\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2},

(5.2)

and, respectively, for the classical color charges we get

\frac{d\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.42271pt\Bigl\{\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}\hskip 0.85355pt,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{H}}^{(5)}\Bigr\}=\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{H}}^{(5)}}{\,\partial\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}}\,f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha},

or

\displaystyle\frac{d\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{1}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\,+\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,c^{\ast\ \!\!(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\biggr],

(5.3)

\displaystyle\frac{d\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{2}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\,+\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,c^{\ast\ \!\!(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\biggr].

(5.4)

In the equations (
5.1
) and (
5.2
) for the bosonic normal variables
c^{\phantom{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}\!\!a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and
c^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
instead of the total Hamiltonian
{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)\!}+{\mathcal{H}}^{(5)}
we have used only the interaction Hamiltonian
{\mathcal{H}}^{(5)}
. The contribution of the new free Hamiltonian
{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)}
, Eq. (
4.4
), to an evolution of soft bosonic excitations was actually taken into account in the definition of the vertex functions
{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}(t)
,
{\mathcal{U}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}(t)
and
{\upphi}^{(\alpha)}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}(t)
, Eqs. (
2.12
) and (
2.18
), respectively. Therefore, accounting
{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)}
on the right-hand sides of (
5.1
) and (
5.2
), as it is not difficult to see, result in a kind of ‘‘double-counting’’.

To construct the required kinetic equation it is convenient to use the multiple-time-scale perturbation expansions
[
47
,
50
,
51
,
52
,
54
]
, which was already mentioned in the Introduction. According to this method the wave field
c^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t)
can be divided into a slowly varying (in time) components
C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)
with a characteristic variation time much larger than
1/\omega_{\rm pl}
and a small, rapidly varying components
\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})
(the value of
\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})
changes appreciably within a time on the order of
1/\omega_{\rm pl}
). Most of the energy in the wave field is contained in the slowly component
C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)
. Let us assume that a similar separation into slow and fast components is valid for color charges
\mathcal{Q}^{\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(t)
of hard particles. These assumptions permits us to write

\displaystyle c^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t)=\varepsilon^{\alpha_{1}}\hskip 0.56917ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\,+\,\varepsilon^{\alpha_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime}),

(5.5a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(t)=\varepsilon^{\beta_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(\tau)\,+\,\varepsilon^{\beta_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(t^{\prime}).

(5.5b)

Here,
\varepsilon
is a dimensionless small parameter describing the magnitude of the nonlinearity. The fast time scale is
t^{\prime}
, and the slow time scale is
\tau
. We consider them to be independent variables. The total time derivative is to be expanded according to

\partial/\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt=\varepsilon^{\gamma_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt(\partial/\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\prime})\,+\,\varepsilon^{\gamma_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt(\partial/\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau).

(5.6)

Unknown exponents
\alpha_{1,2},\,\beta_{1,2}
and
\gamma_{1,2}
on the right-hand sides (
5.5
) and (
5.6
) represent non-negative integers
5
5

5

The condition that the exponents must be integers is most likely unnecessary.
, moreover, the natural requirement here is the fulfillment of inequalities

\alpha_{1}<\alpha_{2},\quad\beta_{1}<\beta_{2},\quad\gamma_{1}<\gamma_{2}.

We will determine these exponents while finding a self-consistent solution to the equations for fast and slow components of the original system (
5.1
) – (
5.4
).

Substituting expansions (
5.5
) and (
5.6
) into (
5.1
) and (
5.3
) we decompose the Hamilton equations into the ones for the fast and slow variables as follows:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\alpha_{2}+\gamma_{1}}\,\frac{\partial\hskip 1.13791pt\hat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt=\hskip 0.85355pt\varepsilon^{2\beta_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt(-\hskip 0.85355pti)\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau),

(5.7)

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\alpha_{1}+\gamma_{2}}\,\frac{\partial\hskip 1.13791ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)}{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}\,=\,\varepsilon^{\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt(-\hskip 0.85355pti)\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\Bigl[{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(t^{\prime})+\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\Bigr],

(5.8)

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\beta_{2}+\gamma_{1}}\,\frac{d\hskip 0.28436pt\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(t^{\prime})}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\prime}}=\varepsilon^{\alpha_{1}+2\beta_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{1}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)

(5.9)

\displaystyle\hskip 241.84842pt+\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,C^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\biggr],

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\beta_{1}+\gamma_{2}}\,\frac{d\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}\,=\varepsilon^{\alpha_{2}+2\beta_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{1}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)

(5.10)

\displaystyle\hskip 241.84842pt+\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\widehat{C}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\biggr]

\displaystyle+\,\varepsilon^{\alpha_{1}+\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{1}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl({\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(t^{\prime})+\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\Bigr)

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt+\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,C^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl({\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(t^{\prime})+\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\Bigr)\biggr].

Let us make a few comments. The effective amplitude
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}
, that is included in the initial dynamic equations
(
5.1
) – (
5.4
), is itself a function of time. We attributed this dependence to the ‘‘fast’’ time
t^{\prime}
. The most problematic in our decomposition is the first term on the right-hand side Eq. (
5.1
) (and, accordingly, in the conjugate equation (
5.2
)). The term of such a kind did not occur in the earlier works on the application of the multiple-time-scale formalism to the construction of kinetic equations. We have placed this contribution only to the equation for the rapidly varying component
\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})
. As will be shown later, this leads to correct final expressions.

The requirement of equality of the left- and right-hand sides of the system (
5.7
) – (
5.10
) with respect to the small parameter
\varepsilon
leads to the following system of algebraic equations for the exponents:

\begin{array}[]{lll}(1)&\alpha_{2}+\gamma_{1}=2\hskip 0.56917pt\beta_{1},&\beta_{2}+\gamma_{1}=\alpha_{1}+2\hskip 0.56917pt\beta_{1},\\[8.61108pt]
(2)&\alpha_{1}+\gamma_{2}=\beta_{1}+\beta_{2},&\beta_{1}+\gamma_{2}=\alpha_{2}+2\beta_{1},\\[8.61108pt]
(3)&\beta_{1}+\gamma_{2}=\alpha_{1}+\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}.&\end{array}

We easily express the solution of the equations in lines (1) and (2) in terms of the exponents
\alpha_{1}
and
\alpha_{2}
:

\beta_{1}=2\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{1},\quad\beta_{2}=\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2},\quad\gamma_{1}=4\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2},\quad\gamma_{2}=2\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}.

(5.11)

The equation in line (3), which is related to the last contribution to (
5.10
), turns into an identity for these solutions.
If one fix values for the exponents
\alpha_{1}
and
\gamma_{1}
, putting, for example,

\alpha_{1}=1,\quad\gamma_{1}=0,

to be as close as possible to the early work
[
54
]
, we further
get

\alpha_{2}=4\quad\beta_{1}=2,\quad\beta_{2}=5,\quad\gamma_{2}=6.

Hence, the representations (
5.5
) and (
5.6
) take the final form

\displaystyle c^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t)=\varepsilon\,C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\,+\,\varepsilon^{4}\hskip 0.85355pt\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(t)=\varepsilon^{2}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(\tau)\,+\,\varepsilon^{5}\hskip 0.56917pt\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\,d}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(t^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\partial/\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt=(\partial/\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\prime})\,+\,\varepsilon^{6}\hskip 0.56917pt(\partial/\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau).

In equations (
5.7
) – (
5.10
) now we can set
\varepsilon=1
. Since the terms involving
C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)

and
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(\tau)
on the right-hand side of equation (
5.9
) do not depend on
t^{\prime}
, we can integrate with respect to
t^{\prime}
to give, to leading order,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(t^{\prime})=f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{1}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\,C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)

(5.12)

\displaystyle+\!

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\,C^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\biggr]

and similarly

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(t^{\prime})=f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{2}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\,C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)

(5.13)

\displaystyle+\!

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\,C^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\biggr].

The integral over
t^{\prime}
is understood as indefinite. We assume the integration constant to be zero without loss of generality. Further, the integration of the equation Eq. (
5.7
) with respect to
t^{\prime}
leads us to the following representation for the rapidly varying components
\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})
:

\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.13791pt\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr){\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau).

(5.14)

6
Kinetic equation for soft gluon excitations

Substituting the solutions obtained (
5.12
) and (
5.13
) in Eq. (
5.8
), we derive the following equation for the slowly varying field component
C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)
:

\frac{\partial\hskip 1.13791ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)}{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}\,=\,(-\hskip 0.85355pti)\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\times\vskip-14.22636pt

(6.1)

\displaystyle\biggl\{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{2}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}_{\;{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\,C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)

\displaystyle+\!

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}_{\;{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\,C^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\biggr]

\displaystyle+\,f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{1}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\,C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)

\displaystyle+\!

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\,C^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\biggr]{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\biggr\}.

Similar equation is valid for the slowly varying conjugate component
C^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)
also. If the ensemble of interacting Bose-excitations at a low nonlinearity level has random phases, then it can be statistically described by introducing the bosonic one-time, two-component correlation function of the following form:

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptC^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\bigr\rangle={\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\!(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}),

(6.2)

where
\langle\cdot\rangle
denotes ensemble averaging and the matrix function
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\!(\tau)
we interpret as the plasmon number density.

Let us define a kinetic equation for the plasmon number density employing the equation (
6.1
) and the definition (
6.2
).
The time evolution of the
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\!(\tau)
is obtained by multiplying Eq. (
6.1
) for
C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)
by
C^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)
, ensemble averaging and adding the Hermitian transpose. The right-hand side of the resulting expression will contain eighth-order correlation functions of the following form:

\begin{split}&\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptC^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\bigr\rangle,\\[6.45831pt]
&\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptC^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\bigr\rangle\end{split}

(6.3)

etc. Further, by differentiating the eighth-order correlation functions with respect to
\tau
with allowance made for (
6.1
), (
5.10
), (
5.12
) – (
5.14
) we derive the equation the right-hand side of which will contain the even higher order correlation functions in the variables
C^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau),\,C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)
and
\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}
.
However, we simplify the task as much as possible. We close the chain of equations at the first stage by expressing the eighth-order correlation functions (
6.3
) in terms of the pair correlation functions for the slowly varying components
C^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)
and
C^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)
, and for the mean value of the slowly varying color charges
\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(\tau)
, i.e. we put

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptC^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\bigr\rangle\simeq

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptC^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\simeq

(6.4)

\displaystyle\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime})\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\!(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

and so on.

The kinetic equation can be presented in a more visual form if we set for the effective amplitude
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})
the following color and momentum decomposition

{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})=f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime}).

(6.5)

After simple algebraic transformations we get the desired matrix-valued kinetic equation

\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}\!\ ^{\prime})\,\frac{\partial\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\!(\tau)}{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=

(6.6)

\displaystyle-\,

\displaystyle{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\rho,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

\displaystyle-\,

\displaystyle{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\rho,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

\displaystyle-\,

\displaystyle{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha^{\prime})}_{\;{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\rho)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

\displaystyle-\,

\displaystyle{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha^{\prime})}_{\;{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\rho)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,

where
(\hskip 0.28436ptT^{\,a})^{\hskip 0.28436ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\equiv-i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}
are generators in the adjoint representation and we have introduced matrix notation with respect to the color indices for the plasmon number density
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}=\bigl({\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\bigr)
. It is recalled that the plasmon number density
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\!(\tau)
is a matrix function of both the ‘‘indices’’
\alpha
and
\alpha^{\prime}
, labeling hard particles under consideration, and the color indices
a
and
a^{\prime}
. Let us consider first the following decomposition of the plasmon number density with respect to
\alpha
and
\alpha^{\prime}
:

{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}=\frac{1}{2}\,\delta^{\,\alpha\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha^{\prime}}\!\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k}+\,\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\boldsymbol{\sigma})^{\alpha\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime}}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,{\mathcal{W}}^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k},

(6.7)

where
\Delta{\mathbf{v}}={\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2}
;
\boldsymbol{\sigma}=(\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2},\sigma_{3})
are the Pauli matrices. However, even such a representation in the case of an arbitrary direction of the velocity difference
\Delta{\mathbf{v}}
of two colliding hard particles leads to highly complicated expressions. Therefore, in order to simplify task as much as possible, we consider that
\Delta{\mathbf{v}}
is directed along the axis
OZ
and then instead of (
6.7
) we have

{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}=\frac{1}{2}\,\delta^{\,\alpha\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha^{\prime}}\!\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k}+\,\frac{1}{2}\,({\sigma}_{3})^{\alpha\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k}.

(6.8)

The first step is to define an equation for the ‘‘trivial’’ part of the plasmon number density, i.e. for
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k}(\tau)
in (
6.6
). For this purpose, we take the trace of the left- and right-hand sides of equation (
6.6
) with respect to the indices
\alpha
and
\alpha^{\prime}
, i.e. we put
\alpha=\alpha^{\prime}
and perform the summation over
\alpha
. Setting
{\bf k}={\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}
, we arrive at the following kinetic equation for the plasmon number density
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k}(\tau)
, instead of (
6.6
):

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\!(\tau)}{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=-\,\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\,

\displaystyle{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\Bigl\{\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\!\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\!\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}\,-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\,

\displaystyle{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt({\rho})}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\Bigl\{\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}\,-

(6.9)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\Bigl\{\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\!\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle{\hskip 241.84842pt}\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\!\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}\,-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\,{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt({\rho})}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\Bigl\{\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle{\hskip 241.84842pt}\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}.

Here, on the right-hand side we have introduced matrix notations
{\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{a_{1}}\!\!\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}=\bigl({\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}}\bigr)
and

{\mathcal{W}}^{\phantom{a_{1}}\!\!\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}=\bigl({\mathcal{W}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}}\bigr)

and we suppressed the slow time
\tau
-dependence of these functions, as well as the average values of the slowly varying components of the color charges
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,\,\alpha=1,2
.

Next, to obtain the kinetic equation for the second part of the plasmon number density, i.e. for
{\mathcal{W}}^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k}(\tau)
we need now to contract the left- and right-hand sides of the equation (
6.6
) with the Pauli matrix
({\sigma}_{3})^{\alpha^{\prime}\hskip 0.28436pt\alpha}
taking into account that
{\rm tr}(\sigma_{3})^{2}=2
. An explicit form of the required equation for the color matrix function
{\mathcal{W}}^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k}(\tau)
is given in Appendix
B
, Eq. (
B.1
).

7
First moment about color of the kinetic equations (
6.9
) and (
B.1
)

Let us consider the following color decomposition of the matrix functions
{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and
{\mathcal{W}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
:

\begin{split}&{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}=\delta^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt+\,\bigl(T^{\,c_{1}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}\!\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k},\\[6.45831pt]
&{\mathcal{W}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}=\delta^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}\hskip 0.28436ptW^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}\!+\,\bigl(T^{\,c_{2}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}\!\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k},\end{split}

(7.1)

where
N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(i)}_{\bf k}
and
W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(i)}_{\bf k},\,i=1,2
, are ordinary numeric functions of the variables
\tau
and
{\bf k}
. In this section we define equations for the colorless parts of the plasmon number density, i.e. for
N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}
and
W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}
. For concreteness, we first define the equation for the function
N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}
. For this purpose, we take the trace of the left- and right-hand sides of equation (
6.9
) with respect to the color indices, i.e. we set
a=a^{\prime}
and perform the summation over
a
. Using the color expansion (
7.1
) and the obvious formula

{\rm tr}\,{\mathcal{N}}_{\bf k}=(N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-1)N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k},

we easily find from (
6.9
)

\displaystyle d_{A}\hskip 1.13791pt\frac{\partial\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=-\,\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\,

\displaystyle{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 128.0374pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}\,-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\,

\displaystyle{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt({\rho})}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 128.0374pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}\,-

(7.2)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle{\hskip 224.7766pt}{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}\,-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\,{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt({\rho})}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle{\hskip 224.7766pt}{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}.

Hereinafter, for simplicity, we omit the prime at the fast time
t^{\prime}
. At the beginning we analyze the coefficient functions related to the product of effective amplitudes
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)
and
{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)
. Here we are interested in the dependence on the fast time
t
.

The first step is to write out again the representation for
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)
in terms of the integral
over the momentum transfer
{\mathbf{q}}
, Eq. (
4.6
),

{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)=\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t).

(7.3)

Due to the structure of the effective amplitude
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)
, as it was defined by expressions (
4.7
) – (
4.9
), dependence on the fast time is included in this amplitude through the vertex functions. Let us consider in the first part of the effective amplitude
{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)
the products of three vertex functions
{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1,2)}(t)
in the square brackets. Using the representation (
2.18
) these products can be written in the following form:

\displaystyle{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}(t)={\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}\,{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{1}+({\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2})\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})+i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf x}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}},

(7.4a)

\displaystyle{\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}(t)={\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}\,{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{1}-({\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2})\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})+i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf x}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}},

(7.4b)

\displaystyle{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}(t)={\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}\,{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{2}-({\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2})\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})+i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf x}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2}},

(7.4c)

\displaystyle{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}(t)={\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}\,{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{2}+({\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2})\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})+i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf x}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2}}.

(7.4d)

From the expressions obtained, we see that they have different exponential multipliers. By performing a change of the integration variable
{\bf q}
, they can be brought to the same form. This replacement depends on the value of the ‘‘index’’
\rho=1,2
. For the amplitude
{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(1)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)
, as a primary expression we take (
7.4a
), and in all terms in (
4.8
) related to the products (
7.4b
) – (
7.4d
), we perform the following substitutions:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{q}}\rightarrow-{\mathbf{q}}\quad\mbox{for (\ref{eq:7tb})},

\displaystyle{\mathbf{q}}\rightarrow{\mathbf{k}}-{\mathbf{q}}\quad\mbox{for (\ref{eq:7tc})},

(7.5)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{q}}\rightarrow-({\mathbf{k}}-{\mathbf{q}})\quad\mbox{for (\ref{eq:7td})}.

On the other hand, for the amplitude
{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{2}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)
, as a primary expression we will use (
7.4c
), and in the remaining terms in (
4.8
) related to the products (
7.4a
), (
7.4b
) and (
7.4d
), we perform the substitutions similar to the previous ones:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{q}}\rightarrow{\mathbf{k}}-{\mathbf{q}}\quad\mbox{for (\ref{eq:7ta})},

\displaystyle{\mathbf{q}}\rightarrow-({\mathbf{k}}-{\mathbf{q}})\quad\mbox{for (\ref{eq:7tb})},

(7.6)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{q}}\rightarrow-{\mathbf{q}}\quad\mbox{for (\ref{eq:7td})}.

We apply completely similar reasoning for the second part of the effective amplitude:
{T}^{\,{\rm II}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)
, Eq. (
4.9
). Using the representations for the vertex functions (
2.12
) and (
2.18
), we obtain the following expressions for the products in (
4.9
):

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}^{\,\ast\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(2)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)={\mathcal{V}}^{\,\ast\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(2)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\,{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{2}-({\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2})\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})+i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf x}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2}},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}^{\,\ast\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(1)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)={\mathcal{V}}^{\,\ast\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(1)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\,{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{1}+({\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2})\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})+i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf x}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}},

\displaystyle\hskip 256.0748pt\ldots

\displaystyle{\mathcal{U}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(2)}_{-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)={\mathcal{U}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(2)}_{-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\,{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{2}+({\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2})\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})+i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf x}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2}},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{U}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(1)}_{-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}(t)={\mathcal{U}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(1)}_{-{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\,{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{1}-({\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2})\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})+i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf x}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}}.

The exponential factors here are exactly the same as in (
7.4
). Using the same reasoning as above, these factors can be reduced to the same form by the replacements of the integration variable
{\bf q}
, Eqs. (
7.5
) or (
7.6
), depending on the value
\rho
.

Taking into account all of the above, we can now explicitly single out the dependence on the fast time
t
(as well as on the initial coordinates of the position of particles 1 and 2) from the amplitude
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)
in the following form:

{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}(t)\,=\,{\rm e}^{-i\hskip 0.85355pt(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}\,-\,(-1)^{\rho}\,({\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2})\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{(-1)^{\rho}\,i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})\,+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917pt\cdot\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf x}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt\rho}}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}},

(7.7)

where now the amplitude
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
on the right side is independent of
t
and have the following structure, instead of (
4.7
) – (
4.9
),

{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}={T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}+{T}^{\,{\rm II}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}},\quad\rho=1,2.

Here, for the first part
{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}},\,\rho=1,2,
we have the final expressions:

\displaystyle{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(1)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf k}}\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl\{\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}\,+

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}\,-\,

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\biggr){\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}\,-

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt({\bf k}-{\bf q})}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt({\bf k}-{\bf q})}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\biggr){\upphi}^{(2)}_{\,-({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,-({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}\biggr\}

and

\displaystyle{T}^{\,{\rm I}\,(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}=i\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf k}}\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl\{\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}\,+
