\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(2)}_{\,-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(2)}_{\,{\bf k}}\,-

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\biggr){\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(2)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}\,-

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt({\bf k}-{\bf q})}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt({\bf k}-{\bf q})}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\biggr){\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,-({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\,-({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{(1)}_{\,{\bf k}}\biggr\}.

The second part
{T}^{\,{\rm II}\,(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}
, connected with the effective three-plasmon vertices, can be presented in a similar form:

\displaystyle T^{\,{\rm II}\,(1)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}=-\hskip-170.71652pt

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt2\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{V}}^{\,\ast\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(1)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\biggr)\,-

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt2\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\ {\bf q},\,{\bf q}-{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(2)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(1)}_{\,{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf q}-{\bf k})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf q}-{\bf k})}\biggr)\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt2\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a_{1}\,a_{2}\,a}_{\ {\bf k}-{\bf q},\,-{\bf q},\,{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(1)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(2)}_{\,-{\bf q}}\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\biggr)\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt2\,{\mathcal{U}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf q}-{\bf k},\,-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(1)}_{\,{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(2)}_{\,-{\bf q}}\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf q}-{\bf k})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf q}-{\bf k})}\biggr)

and

\displaystyle T^{\,{\rm II}\,(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}=\;\hskip-170.71652pt

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt2\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{V}}^{\,\ast\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf q},\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(2)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\biggr)\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt2\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\ {\bf q},\,{\bf q}-{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(1)}_{\,{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(2)}_{\,{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf q}-{\bf k})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf q}-{\bf k})}\biggr)\,-

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt2\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a_{1}\,a_{2}\,a}_{\ {\bf k}-{\bf q},\,-{\bf q},\,{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\,(2)}_{\,{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(1)}_{\,-{\bf q}}\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}-{\bf q}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf q})}\biggr)\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt2\,{\mathcal{U}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,-{\bf q},\,{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(1)}_{\,-{\bf q}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,(2)}_{\,{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot{\bf q}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{-\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}}+{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot{\bf q}}\biggr)\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{1}\cdot({\bf q}-{\bf k})}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf q}-{\bf k}}\!-{\bf v}^{\phantom{l}}_{2}\cdot({\bf q}-{\bf k})}\biggr).

Here we used the antisymmetry property of vertex functions when rearranging arguments, Eq. (
2.17
). By employing the color and momentum factorization for the vertex functions, Eq. (
2.14
), we can write in fully analogy with (
6.5
)

{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}=f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}.

Let us now return to our original kinetic equation (
7.2
). As was mentioned above the fast time integrals on the right-hand side are considered as the indefinite ones. Taking into account the representations (
7.3
) and (
7.7
), we find that

{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)=

(7.8)

-\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\,d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}^{\prime}\;{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\,{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}^{\prime}}\,{\rm e}^{i\hskip 0.85355pt\,(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\mathbf{q}}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{i\,(-1)^{\rho}\,({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})\cdot({\bf q}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})}\,\frac{i}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime}+i0}.

Here, there is no summation over
\rho
is implied and for brevity we introduced the notation

\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\equiv{\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2}.

For convenience, we also provide the complex conjugate expression for (
7.8
), in which, however, we exchange the integration variables
{\mathbf{q}}\rightleftharpoons{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime}
:

{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt({\rho})}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)=

(7.9)

\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\,d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}^{\prime}\;{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\,{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}^{\prime}}\,{\rm e}^{i\hskip 0.85355pt\,(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\mathbf{q}}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{i\,(-1)^{\rho}\,({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})\cdot({\bf q}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})}\,\frac{i}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}-i0}.

With this replacement, the integrand remains unchanged up to the sign (cp. with (
7.8
)), except for the expression in the denominator. For concreteness, let us consider the first two terms on the right-hand side of (
7.2
). Now we consider the expressions for color traces. We assume that the color matrix function
{\mathcal{N}}_{\bf k}
is Hermitian, i.e.

{\mathcal{N}}^{\,\dagger}_{\bf k}={\mathcal{N}}_{\bf k}.

By virtue of the decomposition (
7.1
), this means that the scalar functions
N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}
and
N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}
are real. In this case, for the traces in the second term on the right-hand side of (
7.2
), due to the Hermitian nature of the adjoint representation matrices
T^{\,a}
, the following relations hold

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\ast}={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr),\;{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\ast}={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr).

(7.10)

In general, when using the color decomposition (
7.1
), the traces on the left- and right-hand sides here are complex, i.e. they do not satisfy the relations of the form

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\ast}={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr),\;{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\ast}={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)

(7.11)

etc. However, as we will see below, when contracting these traces with the averaged color charges, as is the case in the kinetic equation (
7.2
), we get real-valued expressions. This leads to the fact that, by virtue of the properties (
7.10
) the following equalities are valid:

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Similar relations hold for the expressions in (
7.2
) with another color matrix function
{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
. This in turn with accounting made for representations (
7.8
), (
7.9
) and the relation (
7.10
) enables us to bring the first two terms on the right-hand side of (
7.2
) to the following form:

\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)+{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt({\rho})}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)\biggr]\hskip 0.85355pt\times

(7.12)

\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]=

-\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\,d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}^{\prime}\;{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\,{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}^{\prime}}\,{\rm e}^{i\hskip 0.85355pt\,(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\mathbf{q}}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{i\,(-1)^{\rho}\,({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})\cdot({\bf q}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})}\,\times

\biggl(\frac{i}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime}+i0}\,-\,\frac{i}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}-i0}\biggr)\times

\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr].

A completely similar expression is true for terms in (
7.2
) with the matrix function
{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
.

For the difference of two terms in parentheses on the right-hand side of (
7.12
), we cannot directly use Sokhotsky’s formula

\frac{i}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}+i0}\,-\,\frac{i}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}-i0}=

(7.13)

2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}),

since the first term there depends on the variable
{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime}
, while the second one depends on
{\mathbf{q}}
. However, the relation (
7.13
) is necessary to obtain the desired kinetic equation. The bremsstrahlung probability must contain a
\delta
-function which describes the conservation of energy in an elementary bremsstrahlung event in the quasiclassical limit
6
6

6

The conservation law

\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}=0

(7.14)

can be derived from the following simple reasoning. Indeed, let the particles 1 and 2 have had momenta
{\mathbf{p}}_{1}
and
{\mathbf{p}}_{2}
before the collision. As a result of the collision, the momentum
{\mathbf{q}}
was transferred from particle 2 to particle 1, and in addition,
a wave with energy
\omega^{l}_{\mathbf{k}}
and momentum
{\mathbf{k}}
was emitted (this wave corresponds to the bremsstrahlung quantum). The final momenta after the collision of the particles are
{\mathbf{p}}^{\prime}_{1}
and
{\mathbf{p}}^{\prime}_{2}
:

{\mathbf{p}}^{\prime}_{1}={\mathbf{p}}_{1}+{\mathbf{q}}-{\mathbf{k}},\quad{\mathbf{p}}^{\prime}_{2}={\mathbf{p}}_{2}-{\mathbf{q}}.

The conservation energy law has the form

\varepsilon({\mathbf{p}}_{1})+\varepsilon({\mathbf{p}}_{2})=\varepsilon({\mathbf{p}}^{\prime}_{1})+\varepsilon({\mathbf{p}}^{\prime}_{2})+\omega^{l}_{\mathbf{k}},

where
\varepsilon({\mathbf{p}})
is energy of the incoming or outgoing hard particles. From here in the limit
|{\mathbf{p}}_{\rho}|\gg|{\mathbf{k}}|,\,|{\mathbf{q}}|,\,\rho=1,2
, we result in (
7.14
) for the value
\rho=1
. If the radiation of the bremsstrahlung quantum was associated with particle 2, then we would arrive at (
7.14
) for the value
\rho=2
.

.

To obtain relation (
7.13
) we use the product of two exponential factors in the integrand on the right-hand side of (
7.12
). The plasmon number density
{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and the averaged color charges generally change slowly compared with the exponential oscillatory factors. We should take the average over these oscillatory factors.
The first step is to integrate over
t
in the first exponential factor that gives

\int\limits^{+\infty}_{-\infty}\!d\hskip 0.56917ptt\,{\rm e}^{i\hskip 0.85355pt\,(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\mathbf{q}}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917ptt}=2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\mathbf{q}}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime}))=2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\,\frac{1}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\delta({q}_{\hskip 0.56917pt\parallel}-{q}^{\prime}_{\hskip 0.56917pt\parallel}),

(7.15)

where
{q}_{\hskip 0.56917pt\parallel}
(and
{q}^{\prime}_{\hskip 0.56917pt\parallel}
) is the longitudinal component of the momentum transfer.

Further in the second exponential factor without loss of generality one can set
{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}
= 0, and choose the vector
{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2}=({\mathbf{b}},z_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})
, where two-dimensional vector
{\mathbf{b}}
is orthogonal to the relative velocity
\Delta{\mathbf{v}}={\mathbf{v}}_{1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{2}
. Besides in the subsequent discussion the longitudinal component
z_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2}
also does not play any role and thus it can be set equal to zero. As a result, here we have

{\rm e}^{\hskip 0.56917pti\,(-1)^{\rho}\,({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})\cdot({\bf q}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})}={\rm e}^{\hskip 0.56917pti\,(-1)^{\rho}\,{\mathbf{b}}\cdot({\bf q}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})_{\perp}}.

(7.16)

The vector
{\mathbf{b}}
plays the role of an impact parameter. Thus the scalar functions
N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(i)}_{\bf k}
and
W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(i)}_{\bf k},\,i=1,2
, in the decompositions (
7.1
) do not depend only on the slow time
\tau
and the wave vector
{\mathbf{k}}
, but also on the two-dimensional vector
{\mathbf{b}}
. Instead of these functions, we introduce their ‘‘averaged’’ versions over the vector
{\mathbf{b}}
, while keeping the same notations

N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(i)}_{\bf k}(\tau)\equiv\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathbf{b}}\,N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(i)}_{\bf k}(\tau;{\mathbf{b}}),\qquad W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(i)}_{\bf k}(\tau)\equiv\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathbf{b}}\,W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(i)}_{\bf k}(\tau;{\mathbf{b}}).

(7.17)

We perform integration over the impact parameter
{\mathbf{b}}
, taking into account the expression (
7.16
) and the definition (
7.17
) and using the approximations

\begin{split}&\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathbf{b}}\,{\rm e}^{\hskip 0.56917pti\,(-1)^{\rho}\,{\mathbf{b}}\cdot({\bf q}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})_{\perp}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}(\tau;{\mathbf{b}})\simeq(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{2}\,\delta^{(2)}(({\mathbf{q}}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})_{\hskip 0.56917pt\perp})\,{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}(\tau),\\[4.30554pt]
&\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathbf{b}}\,{\rm e}^{\hskip 0.56917pti\,(-1)^{\rho}\,{\mathbf{b}}\cdot({\bf q}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})_{\perp}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{{\bf k}}(\tau;{\mathbf{b}})\simeq(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{2}\,\delta^{(2)}(({\mathbf{q}}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})_{\hskip 0.56917pt\perp})\,{\mathcal{W}}_{{\bf k}}(\tau).\end{split}

(7.18)

Eqs. (
7.15
) and (
7.18
) enables us to perform complete integration with respect to
{\bf q}^{\prime}
in (
7.12
). In the end, we obtain instead of (
7.2
),

\displaystyle d_{A}\hskip 1.13791pt\frac{\partial\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times\vskip-14.22636pt

(7.19)

\displaystyle{\hskip-51.21504pt}\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]\,+

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle{\hskip-49.79231pt}\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr].

All that is left to do, is to explicitly calculate the color traces. Using the color decomposition (
7.1
) and the formulae for the traces of the product of three and four color matrices (generators) in the adjoint representation in Appendix
D
, Eqs. (
D.1
) and (
D.2
), we easily find

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)=

(7.20)

N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)+\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)=\frac{i}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,e_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}\,+

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}\hskip 0.56917ptc_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(\hskip 0.56917pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}\hskip 0.56917pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc_{1}}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc_{1}\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.28436ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte}\!+d^{\hskip 1.13791pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc_{1}\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.56917ptd^{\hskip 1.13791pte_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)\Bigr\},

where
d^{\hskip 1.13791pta\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}
are the totally symmetric structure constants in the
\mathfrak{su}(N_{c})
Lie algebra. As mentioned above, this trace is a complex quantity, i.e. it does not satisfy the first relation in (
7.11
). However, this trace enters into the kinetic equation (
7.19
) in the form of contraction with the color charges

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,

and therefore, considering the antisymmetry of the structure constants
f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,e_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}
, we see that the imaginary part of (
7.20
) vanishes. For the real part, we get

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,+\,\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.56917pt-\Lambda^{2}+\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\bigl)\biggr\}N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}.

(7.21)

Here, we set
\Lambda^{2}\equiv\Lambda^{e}\Lambda^{e},\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\equiv\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{12}\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{12}
.
Similarly, for the second trace in (
7.19
) we obtain

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=\biggl\{2\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{11}\hskip 0.85355pt\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{12}\bigl)\biggr\}N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}.\hskip 62.59596pt

(7.22)

On the right-hand sides of Eqs. (
7.21
) and (
7.22
) we have introduced, by the definition, the following functions of the slow time
\tau
:

{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\equiv\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,\quad{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\equiv\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,\quad{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\equiv\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,\vskip-8.5359pt

(7.23)

\displaystyle\Lambda^{c}=\Lambda^{c}(\tau)\equiv f^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\,b_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,b_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,b_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,

\displaystyle\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{11}=\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{11}(\tau)\equiv d^{\,c\,b_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,b_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,b^{\prime}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,

\displaystyle\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{22}=\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{22}(\tau)\equiv d^{\,c\,b_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptb^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,b_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,b^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,

\displaystyle\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{12}=\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{12}(\tau)\equiv d^{\,c\,b_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,b_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,b^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

We see that in the case of an arbitrary value of
N_{c}
, the number of color structures associated with the color charges is too large. To minimize their number, we will consider specific values for
N_{c}
. For the ‘‘trivial’’ case
\mathfrak{su}(2_{\hskip 0.56917ptc})
, when
d^{\,a\,b\hskip 0.85355ptc}\!\equiv 0
the last three color structure in (
7.23
) vanish. However, as is shown in Appendix
E
, it is possible to completely get rid of these color structures (or rather, from their colorless combinations such as
\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}=\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{12}\hskip 0.85355pt\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{12}
and
\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{11}\hskip 0.85355pt\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{12}
) for another, more physically important case of the
\mathfrak{su}(3_{c})
Lie algebra. For
N_{c}=3
, by virtue of the relations (
E.3
) and (
E.5
), the following two equalities are true

\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}=\frac{1}{3}\,\bigl({\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}-\Lambda^{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\quad\mbox{and}\quad\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{11}\hskip 0.85355pt\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{12}=\frac{1}{3}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}.

With these equalities at hand we have, instead of the expressions (
7.21
) and (
7.22
),

\begin{split}&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k},\\[4.30554pt]
&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}.\end{split}

(7.24)

The relative simplicity of these latter expressions stems from our choice of the color decomposition (
7.1
). With any other, a more sophisticated choice of the representations (
7.1
), expressions for the color traces become noticeably more complicated.

For the traces in (
7.19
) with the matrix function
{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
the equalities (
7.24
) take the form

\begin{split}&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k},\\[4.30554pt]
&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}.\end{split}

Let us now write the final kinetic equation for the function
N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}
, instead of (
7.19
),

\displaystyle d_{A}\hskip 1.13791pt\frac{\partial\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=\;

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times\vskip-14.22636pt

(7.25)

\displaystyle\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}+\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt\,+

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}+\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}\hskip 0.56917ptW^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}.

Reasoning fully analogous with those used above, leads us to the kinetic equation for the second ‘‘colorless’’ part
W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}
in the representation (
7.1
):

\displaystyle d_{A}\hskip 1.13791pt\frac{\partial\hskip 0.28436ptW^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=\,

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times\vskip-14.22636pt

(7.26)

\displaystyle\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}+\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}\hskip 0.56917ptW^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt\,+

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}+\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}.

It follows from the equation for the color matrix function
{\mathcal{W}}^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k}(\tau)
, Eq. (
B.1
).

We can propose a slightly different representation for the plasmon density matrix as an alternative to existing representations (
6.8
) and (
7.1
), without using the matrix
\sigma_{3}
, namely

{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)=N^{(0)}_{\bf k}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptI^{(1)}\otimes I^{(2)}+N^{(1)}_{\bf k}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{(1)\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}\otimes I^{(2)}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+N^{(2)}_{\bf k}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptI^{(1)}\otimes T^{(2)\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

N^{(12)}_{\bf k}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{(1)\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}\otimes T^{(2)\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc}\Lambda^{c}(\tau),

(7.27)

where
I^{(1,\hskip 0.85355pt2)}
are the identity matrices of dimension
(N^{2}_{c}-1)\times(N^{2}_{c}-1)
and
\otimes
is the tensor product symbol. We associate own color space with each of two color particles, as indicated by the labels 1 or 2 in parentheses. On the left-hand side of (
7.27
) the matrix function
{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
has the four-indices color structure
{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}=(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a_{1}a_{2},\,a^{\prime}_{1}a^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt)
. It can be related in a certain way to the plasmon density matrix
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\alpha,\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
, introduced by us because of the definition of the correlation function (
6.2
) and, thereby, connect the four scalar functions included in the color decomposition (
7.1
) with the four functions from the representation (
7.27
).

8
Second moment about color of the kinetic equation (
6.9
)

Let us return to the matrix kinetic equation (
6.9
). Now we contract the left- and right-hand sides of this equation with the color matrix
(\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s_{1}})^{\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}\hskip 0.28436pta}
.
Using the color decomposition (
7.1
) and the formula

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)=N_{c}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k},

as a result, from (
6.9
) we get

N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt\frac{\partial\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl(\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}\bigr)}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=

(8.1)

\displaystyle-\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 162.18062pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}\,-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\,{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt({\rho})}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle\hskip 162.18062pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}\,-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+\,

\displaystyle\hskip 162.18062pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}\,-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\,{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt({\rho})}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\,\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptdt\biggr)\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+\,

\displaystyle\hskip 162.18062pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}.

The completely analogous reasoning that brought us from equation (
7.2
) to equation (
7.19
) is also true here. Therefore, omitting the intermediate considerations, we immediately give the necessary equation, which follows from (
8.1
)

N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt\frac{\partial\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl(\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}\bigr)}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times\vskip-14.22636pt

(8.2)

\displaystyle\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]\,+

\displaystyle{\hskip 133.72786pt}\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr].

Just like in case of deriving kinetic equations (
7.25
) and (
7.26
), from (
8.2
) we can obtain the desired kinetic equation for the second scalar function
N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}
with the correct conservation law only if the contractions of the type

\begin{split}&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,\\[4.30554pt]
&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\end{split}

(8.3)

and so on, are real functions. Let us consider the color traces for the first contraction in (
8.3
). By taking into account the representation (
7.1
), we have

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)=N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)+\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr).

Here, the fourth-order trace of the adjoint representation matrices
T^{a}
is real, and the fifth-order trace is purely imaginary. We will show that the contraction of the last trace with the averaged color charges, as occurs in (
8.3
) does indeed vanish. The easiest way to show this is to use the relation (
D.7
) for the fifth-order trace. After relabel of the dummy summation indices, where it is needed, this gives us

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,-\,

i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pts_{1}\,c_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

or

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

(8.4)

-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\,f^{\hskip 0.85355pts_{1}\,c_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

From the last equality, we see that unlike (
7.20
), (
7.21
) here the fifth-order trace of the
T^{\,a}
matrices doesn’t automatically disappear when contracting with the averaged color charges, since

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)

by virtue of the property (
D.5
). It is evident that the expression (
8.4
) can be turned to zero if one contracts it with the
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, i.e.,

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=0.

(8.5)

By virtue of the above, equation (
8.2
) will hold true only when contracting it with the averaged color charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. Therefore, instead of (
8.2
) we should write

N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,\frac{\partial\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl(\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}\bigr)}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=\vskip-8.5359pt

(8.6)

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]\,+

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr].

Taking into account the color decomposition (
7.1
), the equality (
8.5
) and the formula for the trace of fourth order (
D.2
) it is easy to find that for the sum of the traces in the first square bracket on the right-hand side of the kinetic equation (
8.6
), the following representation holds

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

\displaystyle\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}+\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}.

We get a completely similar expression for the sum of the traces in the last two lines in (
8.6
) with the replacement:

N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}\rightarrow W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}
.
Thus, we can write out in addition to (
7.25
) and (
7.26
) the third kinetic equation for the scalar function
N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}
, which we present as follows:

N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\,\frac{\partial\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\,N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=\hskip 28.45274pt

(8.7)

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}+\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt\,+

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.56917pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}+\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}\hskip 0.56917ptW^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}.

We just need to write out the equation for the four and final scalar function
W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}
. Using the same reasoning as above, here we get from Eq. (
B.1
)

N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,\frac{\partial\hskip 0.28436ptW^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\,W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)}_{\bf k}\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=\hskip 28.45274pt

(8.8)

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}+\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}\hskip 0.56917ptW^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt\,+

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\biggl\{\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}+\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,-\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1)}_{\bf k}.

However, the system of kinetic equations obtained in this and the preceding sections is still unclosed. It contains four unknown functions:
{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(\tau),\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(\tau),\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}(\tau)
and
\Lambda^{2}(\tau)
, as they were defined by the relations (
7.23
). In addition, another circumstance should be mentioned. The right-hand side of the equations (
7.25
), (
7.26
), (
8.7
) and (
8.8
) according to the construction within the framework of the multi-time formalism, contains terms linear in the scalar plasmon number densities
N^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1,2)}_{\bf k}
and
W^{\hskip 0.85355pt(1,2)}_{\bf k}
and it does not contain any terms that are free from them, i.e. proportional only to the products of the averaged color charges. It is not entirely clear why such contributions do not occur here. Indeed, let us consider the correlation function of the original normal variables
c^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t)
and
c^{\ast\ \!\!(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t)
before their decomposition into slow and fast variables. Taking into account the representation (
5.5a
), we have

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptc^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t)\bigr\rangle=\varepsilon^{2\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{1}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptC^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\bigr\rangle\,+\,\varepsilon^{2\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{2}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\widehat{C}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pt\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\bigr\rangle.

Here, on the right-hand side we have assumed that all the correlation functions mixed the slow and fast components, are equal to zero. Define the derivative of
of this equality with respect to the original time
t
. Using the representation (
5.6
) for the total time variation
\partial/\partial\hskip 0.56917ptt
, we derive

\frac{\partial}{\partial\hskip 0.56917ptt}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptc^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t)\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t)\bigr\rangle=

(8.9)

\varepsilon^{2\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{1}+\gamma_{2}}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial\hskip 0.56917pt\tau}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptC^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(\tau)\bigr\rangle\,+\,\varepsilon^{2\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{2}+\gamma_{1}}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\prime}}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\widehat{C}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pt\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\bigr\rangle.

When choosing solutions (
5.11
) for the exponents of the two contributions, on the right-hand side of the previous expression we have

2\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{1}+\gamma_{2}=4\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2},\qquad 2\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{2}+\gamma_{1}=4\hskip 0.56917pt\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2},

that is, these contributions have the same power of the small parameter
\varepsilon
. The first term on the right-hand side of (
8.9
) with the slow time derivative gives us a system of kinetic equations (
7.25
), (
7.26
), (
8.7
) and (
8.8
). The second term with the fast time derivative is easiest to determine using first the initial equation (
5.7
), which gives us

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\prime}}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\widehat{C}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pt\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\bigr\rangle\,=\,i\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\phantom{l}}_{\alpha}\!\cdot{\mathbf{k}}-(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\phantom{l}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\!\cdot{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\widehat{C}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pt\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\bigr\rangle\,+

(8.10)

\displaystyle i\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\mathbf{k}}}(t^{\prime})\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\bigr\rangle-i\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}\,a^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\;{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\widehat{C}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\bigr\rangle

and then substituting the explicit form of the rapidly varying component
\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})
, as defined by Eq. (
5.14
). Formally, the fast time derivative (
8.10
) will be proportional to the product of the four averaged slowly varying color charges, just how we wanted it. However, the physical meaning of the additional contribution (
8.10
) remains very vague for us and we will not take it into account in the kinetic equations obtained above.

9
Equations for the expected value of color charges
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}
and
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}

In this section we consider the derivation of the equations of motion for the expected value of the slowly varying color charges
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}
and
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}
. To be more specific, consider the detailed derivation of the equation for the averaged color charge
\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle
.

Our first step is to average equation (
5.10
). Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{d\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}\,=\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{1}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,
