\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle

(9.1)

\displaystyle+\!

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\widehat{C}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\biggr]

\displaystyle+\,f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte_{1}}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptC^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl({\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(t^{\prime})+\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\bigr)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle

\displaystyle+\!

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.85355pta\,c^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436ptC^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl({\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(t^{\prime})+\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\bigr)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\biggr].

The next step is to put the representations for the rapidly varying field component
\widehat{C}^{\hskip 0.56917pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.56917pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})
, Eq. (
5.14
), and for the rapidly varying color charges
\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}(t^{\prime})
, Eqs. (
5.12
) and (
5.13
), into the right-hand side of equation (
9.1
). Finally, the third and last step is to use approximations of the form (
6.4
) for the correlation functions of the slowly varying variables. Taking into account the color and momentum decomposition of the effective amplitude
{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\alpha)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})
, Eq. (
6.5
), as a result we have, instead of (
9.1
),

\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=

(9.2)

\displaystyle-\,\sum_{\rho,\,\rho^{\prime}}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho^{\prime})}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\Bigl[

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\rho^{\prime},\hskip 0.85355pt\rho)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,-

\displaystyle i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\,

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\rho^{\prime},\hskip 0.85355pt\rho)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,-

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\rho,\hskip 0.85355pt\rho^{\prime})}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\,

\displaystyle\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)-\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\biggr]\hskip 0.85355pt\times

\displaystyle\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Let us make a special note of the fact that the last contribution on the right-hand side of (
9.2
) does not depend at all on the plasmon number density
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\rho^{\prime},\hskip 0.85355pt\rho)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)
.
The physical meaning of this contribution is not entirely clear to us. However, as will be shown in the following discussion, the terms of such a kind vanish in all the dynamic equations relevant to us for the colorless combinations (
7.23
). The reason for this lies in the fact that the color factors of these contributions, which is not related to the dynamics of the system, become zero.

Now we use for the plasmon number density
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,(\rho^{\prime},\hskip 0.85355pt\rho)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}(\tau)
the representation (
6.8
). Substituting this representation into the right-hand side of Eq. (
9.2
) and summing over
\rho^{\prime}
, we derive

\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=

(9.3)

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\Bigl[

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,-

\displaystyle i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07182pt\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\Bigl[

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\,

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\sum_{\rho}\,(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\biggl(\int{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(

\displaystyle t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,-

\displaystyle i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\,

\displaystyle t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Similar equation can be obtained for the second averaged color charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. The form of this equation is given in Appendix
C
, Eq. (
C.1
). The right-hand side of the resulting equation (
9.3
) is real by the construction and has a very complex structure. We are interested in the contributions on the right-hand side of (
9.3
) related only to the plasmon bremsstrahlung process. This means that the integrands must contain the Dirac delta function

2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}),

reflecting the energy-momentum conservation law in the bremsstrahlung process, as it takes place in the kinetic equations (
7.25
), (
7.26
), (
8.7
) and (
8.8
). Repeating the reasoning of two previous sections
in deriving these kinetic equations, we can represent (
9.3
) in the following form:

\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=\vskip-14.22636pt

(9.4)

\displaystyle-\hskip 0.85355pt

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,-

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle\ldots\,.

The dots on the right side denotes the last contribution to (
9.3
), which is independent of the plasmon number densities
{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and
{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
. We analyze this contribution just below. Equation (
9.4
), like equation (
8.2
), is valid only if the contractions of the type

\begin{split}&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,\\[4.30554pt]
i\hskip 0.85355pt&f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\end{split}

(9.5)

and so on, are real functions. In the general case, these contractions are complex and therefore the equation (
9.4
), as such, does not occur. However, we are only interested in the specific colorless combinations
{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(\tau),\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(\tau),\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}(\tau)
and
\Lambda^{2}(\tau)
of the averaged color charges, as were defined by relations (
7.23
), for which the imaginary parts of the contractions of the type (
9.5
) can be zero.

Let us define the equation for the first colorless combination
{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(\tau)=\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. The first step is to substitute the color decomposition for the matrix function
{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
, Eq. (
7.1
), into the first contraction in (
9.5
). As a result we have

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)={N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)+\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt{N}^{(2)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr).

(9.6)

The first term on the right-hand side is real, and the second is purely imaginary. We show that the second term in contracting with
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
turns to zero. For this purpose we use the relation for the fifth-order trace (
D.7
). By virtue of this relation, the following equality is valid

\begin{split}&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=\\[4.30554pt]
-\hskip 0.85355pt&\frac{i}{2}\,f^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}T^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.\end{split}

(9.7)

It is obvious from this expression that when it is contracted with
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, it turns to zero due to the antisymmetry of the structural constants
f^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb}
. Thus, the imaginary part of the first expression in (
9.5
) when it is contracted with
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
vanishes.

Now let us analyze the real part in (
9.6
), that is, the term with the scalar function
{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
. Using the formula for the fourth-order trace (
D.2
), we obtain

{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(9.8)

={N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}+{\mathfrak{q}}^{2}_{12}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{\hskip 0.85355pt11}\hskip 0.85355pt\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{\hskip 0.85355pt22}\biggr\},

where the functions
\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{\hskip 0.85355pt11}
and
\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{\hskip 0.85355pt22}
were defined in (
7.23
). Next, we will consider the special case – the
SU(3_{c})
color group. Then, by virtue of the formula (
E.4
), the previous equality takes the form

{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(9.9)

={N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,+\,\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr\}.

Next, we consider the second expression in (
9.5
), which, after substitution (
7.1
) is rewritten as follows:

\displaystyle i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

(9.10)

\displaystyle i\hskip 0.85355pt{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+

\displaystyle i\hskip 0.85355pt{N}^{(2)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

The first term on the right-hand side is real, and the second is purely imaginary. Obviously, both the real and imaginary parts vanish when they are contracted with
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
.

Completely similar reasoning is valid for the contractions in equation (
9.4
) containing the color matrix function
{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
. Using the color decomposition (
7.1
), we get an analogue of the formula (
9.9
) with the replacement
{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\rightarrow{W}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
. As a result, considering all of the above, after contracting the equation (
9.4
) with

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
we find the desired equation for the first colorless combination
{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(\tau)
:

\displaystyle\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(\tau)}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=-\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,+

(9.11)

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt{W}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\biggr\}\,\times

\,\biggl(\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,+\,\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr)\,+\\
\ldots\,.

The dots on the right-hand side indicates the last contribution to (
9.3
) that does not depend on the scalar plasmon number densities
{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
,
{W}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}},\ldots,
and with which we are now dealing.

Let us analyze the dependence on the fast time of the contribution on the right-hand side of (
9.3
) that does not include the plasmon number density. The obtained expressions (
7.8
) and (
7.9
) enable us to rewrite the last contribution in (
9.3
) in the following form:

\displaystyle\sum_{\rho}\,\biggl[\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)-\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\biggl(\int{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k}}(t^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptdt^{\prime}\biggr)\biggr]\hskip 0.85355pt\times

(9.12)

\displaystyle\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

-\hskip 0.85355pti\,\sum_{\rho}\,\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\,d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}^{\prime}\;{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\,{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}^{\prime}}\,{\rm e}^{i\hskip 0.85355pt\,(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\mathbf{q}}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})\hskip 0.56917ptt}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm e}^{i\,(-1)^{\rho}\,({\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0\hskip 0.56917pt2})\cdot({\bf q}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime})}\,\times

\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime}+i0}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}-i0}\biggr)\times

\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Note that the sign in parentheses on the right-hand side has changed to the opposite of that in the corresponding expression in (
7.12
).

Next, we proceed as in section
7
. The first step is to perform the integration over the fast time
t^{\prime}
, using the formula (
7.15
). Then we introduce into consideration the impact parameter
{\mathbf{b}}
, according to the expression (
7.16
), and average over it. As a result, we obtain in the integrand of (
9.12
) the
\delta
-function of the following form:

(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}\,\frac{1}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\delta({\mathbf{q}}-{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime}),

which in turn enables us to perform the integration over
{\mathbf{q}}^{\prime}
. Thus, instead of (
9.12
) we further obtain

-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.56917pt(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}\,\frac{1}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\sum_{\rho}\,\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\Bigl|{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\Bigr|^{2}\,\times

(9.13)

\biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}+i0}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}-i0}\biggr)\times

\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

-\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.13791pt\,\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\sum_{\rho}\,\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\Bigl|{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\Bigr|^{2}\,\frac{\mathcal{P}}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}}\,\times

\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

Here, at the last stage, the Sokhotsky formula was used and the symbol
\mathcal{P}
denotes the principle value. It should be added to the right-hand side of equation (
9.4
). However, it is easy to verify that in the case of equation for the colorless combination
{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(\tau)
, Eq. (
9.11
), the contribution (
9.13
) is equal to zero. This immediately follows from a trivial analysis of the contraction

\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

\bigl[i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917pte_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr]\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436ptb\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=0.

At the end of this section, we will proceed to the derivation of an equation for somewhat more complex colorless combination of the mixed type, namely
{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\equiv\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. It is convenient to represent the derivative of the function
{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}
with respect to the slow time
\tau
in the following form:

\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}(\tau)}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}\,+\,\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}.

(9.14)

Now we are able to analyze only the first term on the right-hand side. Analysis of the second term, as well as the derivation of equations for the remaining colorless combinations
{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}
and
\Lambda^{\!\hskip 0.28436pt2}
will be performed in the following section.

In order to determine
\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle/{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}\bigr)
, we contract our original equation (
9.4
) with
\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle
. As a first step, take a look at the imaginary part on the right-hand side of (
9.6
). According to formulas (
9.6
) and (
9.7
) for this imaginary part, taking into account the expression for the fourth-order trace (
D.2
), we have the following chain of equalities

-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,{N}^{(2)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}T^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,{N}^{(2)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl[f^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb}\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]\,\biggl\{\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,b}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\,\Bigl(\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,b}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\Bigr)\,+

\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.28436ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+\,d^{\,b\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.28436ptd^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr)\!\biggr\}=

-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,{N}^{(2)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{b}\biggl\{\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{3}{2}\,\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,b}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\,+

\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt(f^{\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.28436ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+\,d^{\,b\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.28436ptd^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle)\biggr\}=

-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{8}\,{N}^{(2)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(-f^{\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{b}\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{e}\,+\,d^{\,b\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{b}\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{12}\biggr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{8}\,{N}^{(2)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,b\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte}\Lambda^{b}\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{12}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Here, for the sake of simplicity, we have suppressed the dependence of the averaged color charges on the slow time
\tau
, taken into account the definition of the functions
\Lambda^{a}
and
\Omega^{a}_{12}
, Eq. (
7.23
), and the quite obvious equality

\Lambda^{a}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=\Lambda^{a}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=0.

From the above expression, we see that in the case of an arbitrary group
SU(N_{c})
, this imaginary part is generally not equal to zero. Apparently, it vanishes for the ‘‘trivial’’ case of
N_{c}=2
, when
d^{\,b\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte}\equiv 0
. However, as was shown in Appendix
E
, Eq. (
E.7
), for the case when
N_{c}=3
, the imaginary part is also zero. Thus, unlike the equation for the colorless combination

{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(\tau)=\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, where the imaginary part (
9.7
) trivially reduced to zero for an arbitrary
N_{c}
, for the colorless combination
{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}(\tau)=\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
we are dealing with a more difficult situation, with specific values
N_{c}
.

Let us now consider the real part for the first expression in (
9.5
). In the case under consideration, instead (
9.8
) and (
9.9
), we now have

{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(9.15)

={N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{2\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,+\,\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{\hskip 0.85355pt22}\hskip 0.85355pt\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{\hskip 0.85355pt12}\biggr\}\bigg|_{N_{c}=3}=\frac{9}{4}\,{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}.

Here, in the last step, we used the relation (
E.5
), which is valid for
N_{c}=3
. We will further examine the imaginary part of the second relation in (
9.5
). According to the formula (
9.10
), this imaginary part is proportional to

{N}^{(2)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Contracting the previous expression with
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
and using (
D.2
) for the color trace, we find that this imaginary part is equal to

\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\,{N}^{(2)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,b_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}\Lambda^{c}\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pte}_{12}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,b_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

As shown in Appendix
E
, Eq. (
E.7
), for the case when
N_{c}=3
, this combination is zero. Thus, the imaginary part for the second relation in (
9.5
) does not contribute to the desired equation for
{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}(\tau)
.

Next, for the real part of the second relation in (
9.5
), using the third-order trace (
D.1
) and contracting with

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, we have

i\hskip 0.85355pt{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

(9.16)

\frac{i^{\,2}}{2}\,{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}^{\prime\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\equiv

\frac{1}{2}\,{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{e}\Lambda^{e}\big|_{N_{c}=3}=\frac{3}{2}\,{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{2}.

Completely similar reasonings are valid for the contractions in equation (
9.4
) containing the matrix function
{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
. Using the color decomposition (
7.1
), we obtain the analogues of Eqs. (
9.15
) and (
9.16
) with the only replacement
{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\rightarrow{W}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
. As a result, taking into account all of the above, after contracting the equation (
9.4
) with

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, we derive the required equation for the first term on the right-hand side of equality (
9.14
):

\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=

(9.17)

\displaystyle-\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,+

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt{W}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\biggr\}\,\times

\,\biggl(\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,-\,\frac{3}{2}\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr).

The contribution of the term (
9.13
) in this equation vanishes by virtue of the fact that the color factor here is zero due to the equality

\bigl[\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr]\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}^{\prime}\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl[\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}^{\prime}\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}^{\prime}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}^{\prime\prime}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr]=-\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}^{\prime}\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{b}\Lambda^{d}\equiv 0.

10
Equations for the expected value of color charges
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\alpha}
(continuation)

In this section, we proceed with the derivation of equations related to the evolution of the rest of the colorless combinations of two averaged color charges. For the special case of the group
SU(3_{c})
, it remains for us to define the equations for the combinations
{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}=\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}=\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
and
\Lambda^{2}=\Lambda^{e}\Lambda^{e}
. To do this, we need to know the equation for the second averaged color charge

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, in addition to equation (
9.4
). The original equation here is (
C.1
) from Appendix
C
. Using the same line of reasoning that led us from (
9.3
) to (
9.4
), we find from (
C.1
)

\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=

(10.1)

\displaystyle-\hskip 0.85355pt

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,-

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{2\hskip 0.85355pt|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,\times

\displaystyle\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr]\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,-

-\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.13791pt\,\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\sum_{\rho}\,\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\Bigl|{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\Bigr|^{2}\,\frac{\mathcal{P}}{\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}}}\,\times

\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

As well as equation (
9.4
) for the averaged color charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr\rangle
, this equation is valid only under the condition that the contractions of the type

\begin{split}&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,\\[4.30554pt]
i\hskip 0.85355pt&f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{2}}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\end{split}

(10.2)

and so on, are real functions. As we have already discussed in two previous sections, in the general case this circumstance does not occur. But we are interested in specific colorless combinations for which this would be true. The first such combination is
{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}=\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. In complete analogy with the derivation of Eq. (
9.11
) for
{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}
, we obtain for
{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}

\displaystyle\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(\tau)}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=-\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,+

(10.3)

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt{W}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\biggr\}\,\times

\,\biggl(\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,+\,\frac{3}{2}\,{\mathfrak{q}}^{2}_{12}\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr).

Here, the last term in (
10.1
) also completely falls out of the dynamics. Note that the right-hand side of (
10.3
) exactly coincides with those of the equation for the function
{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(\tau)
, Eq. (
9.11
). This means that there is the following relationship between the two functions
{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(\tau)
and
{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(\tau)
:

{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(\tau)-{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(\tau)=C,

(10.4)

where
C
is a certain constant that we assume to be equal to

C={\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(0)-{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}(0)\equiv{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}^{0}-{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}^{0}.

Further, the equation (
10.1
) allows us to find the second term in the derivative of the function
{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}(\tau)
, Eq. (
9.14
). As an analogue to the relation (
9.17
), we now have the following expression

\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=

\displaystyle-\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,+

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt{W}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\biggr\}\,\times

\,\biggl(\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}-\,\frac{3}{2}\,\Lambda^{2}\biggr).

Here, the contribution of the last term from (
10.1
) is also zero. Adding the expression above with (
9.17
), we find the desired equation for
the third colorless combination
{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}(\tau)
:

\displaystyle\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}(\tau)}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=-\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\,

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\,+

(10.5)

\displaystyle\frac{(2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi)^{3}}{|\Delta{\mathbf{v}}|}\,\sum_{\rho}\hskip 0.56917pt(-1)^{\rho+1}\!\!

\displaystyle\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf q}\;\bigl|\hskip 0.85355pt{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(\rho)}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf q}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt{W}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\bf k}\cdot{\bf v}_{\rho}-(-1)^{\rho}\,\Delta{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{q}})\biggr\}\,\times

\,\biggl(\frac{9}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\,({\mathfrak{q}}_{1}+{\mathfrak{q}}_{2})-3\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{2}\biggr).

It remains for us to determine the equation for the most complicated colorless function

\Lambda^{\!2}=\Lambda^{e}\Lambda^{e}=\Lambda^{e}f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

The derivative for this function has the following form:

\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{\!\hskip 0.56917pt2}(\tau)}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=2\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{e}(\tau)\,\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{e}(\tau)}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}=

(10.6)

2\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{e}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\biggl\{\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}\,+\,\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}\biggr\}.

First, let us look at the contribution related to the first term in the curly bracket, namely

\Lambda^{e}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}.

(10.7)

We use the equation for the color charge
\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle
, Eq. (
9.4
). Let us analyze the real contributions to (
10.7
). As usual, the first step is to consider the contraction with the trace in the first term on the right-hand side of (
9.6
), which looks like this:

{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{e}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

(10.8)

{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl({\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,\Lambda^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}d^{\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pts}\Lambda^{e}\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pts}_{22}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr).

Here we have again employed the expression for the fourth-order trace and the definition of the function
\Lambda^{e}
. Let us analyze the last term on the right-hand side of this relation for the special case of the
SU(3_{c})
color group. For this purpose, we use equality (
D.9
), which gives us

d^{\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pts}\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pts}_{22}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=d^{\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pts}d^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}^{\prime\prime}\hskip 0.85355pts}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

\frac{1}{3}\,\bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\,a^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{s}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+2\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr].

With the use of this relationship, the last term on the right-hand side of (
10.8
) for
N_{c}=3
takes the form

\frac{3}{4}\,f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}d^{\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pts}\Lambda^{e}\,\Omega^{\hskip 0.85355pts}_{22}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=-\frac{1}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,\Lambda^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr)\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr),

and the whole expression (
10.8
) is written in its final form

{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{e}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

(10.9)

{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}\,\Lambda^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr)\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e^{\prime}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr)\biggr).

Note that a new colorless structure has appeared here, which is no longer reduced to the previously introduced colorless ones
{\mathfrak{q}}_{1},\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2},\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{12}
and
\Lambda^{2}
, even for the
N_{c}=3
case.

Let us now consider another real contribution related to the second contraction in (
9.5
). Here, we have

i\hskip 0.85355pt{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{e}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

(10.10)

-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\Lambda^{e}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=

-\,\frac{1}{2}\,{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr)\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e^{\prime}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr),

where we have used the definition of the function
\Lambda^{e^{\prime}}
.

Completely similar reasoning is valid for the contractions in equation (
9.4
) containing the matrix function
{\mathcal{W}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
. Using the color decomposition (
7.1
), we get an analog of the formula (
10.9
) and (
10.10
) with the replacement
{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\rightarrow{W}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
.

We now need to find similar contributions for the second term in the curly bracket of equality (
10.6
):

\Lambda^{e}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau}.

(10.11)

We will not repeat the calculations performed above, but give the final expression immediately. An analogue of the formula (
10.9
) now is

{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(\frac{3}{4}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}\,\Lambda^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr)\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}\,a_{2}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e^{\prime}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr)\biggr),

(10.12)

and an analogue of the formula (
10.10
) is

-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr)\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}\,e_{2}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e^{\prime}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr).

(10.13)

It remains for us to analyze the contribution that does not depend on the scalar plasmon number densities
{N}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
,
{W}^{(1)}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}},\ldots
to the derivative (
10.6
). We are looking at the term in (
10.6
) associated with the derivative
d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt1}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle/d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau
. Using the color factor for the contribution (
9.13
), we now have the following expression:

\Lambda^{e}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}}_{1}(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\equiv

\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\,a_{2}\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr)\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte_{1}\hskip 0.28436pts\hskip 0.85355pts^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e_{1}}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr)\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}\hskip 0.28436pts^{\prime}\hskip 0.28436pta^{\prime}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\Lambda^{e^{\prime}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a^{\prime}_{2}}_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr)=0.

Here, we have again used the definition of the function
\Lambda^{e^{\prime}}
. A similar contribution from the derivative
d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\mathcal{Q}^{\,a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt2}(\tau)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle/d\hskip 0.85355pt\tau
, as defined in the equation (
10.1
) can also be reduced to a similar form and it is equal to
